- **repeated-abrupt-termination-in-eks-v28-50b7153ecc5bbbe6**: Shows how to fix repeated abrupt termination in EKS, v28.0 due to bad DNS. Use it when you hit this exact problem. Skip it when your error message or symptom looks different. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/repeated-abrupt-termination-in-eks-v28-50b7153ecc5bbbe6))
- **repeated-attempts**: Instructions for reviewing identical failed retries in upstream event logs, accounting for context and fingerprint changes and excluding declared transient errors. Verify the installed detector and evidence before treating a retry as redundant. ([zedarvates/frugal-skills](https://github.com/zedarvates/frugal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/repeated-attempts) · ★1)
- **repeated-clustering-run-aggregation**: Use when a stochastic deep-clustering pipeline (e.g., scDCC with random initialization) yields run-to-run variability and you must quantify whether a modification (e.g., adding pairwise constraints) consistently improves a downstream metric such as cluster-specificity scores of marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/repeated-clustering-run-aggregation))
- **repeated-component-alignment**: Cards, rows, tiles and cells are fixed slot models that stay aligned when content length varies. Use when building or reviewing any repeated component. ([dembrandt/dembrandt-skills](https://github.com/dembrandt/dembrandt-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/repeated-component-alignment) · ★196 · 849 installs · 9 copies)
- **repeated-component-alignment**: Any component rendered many times — cards, list rows, table cells, nav items, tiles, KPI widgets, feed entries — is a fixed slot model, not a free-form box. The same slots appear in the same place in every instance and stay aligned across siblings even when text and values vary in length. Reserve space for optional slots, pin anchor elements (CTA, price, value), clamp overflowing text, and give the full value back via title/tooltip. Use when building or reviewing any repeated component whose content length differs between instances. ([antonyanbu25/lionpath_V2](https://github.com/antonyanbu25/lionpath_V2/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/repeated-component-alignment) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **repeated-cross-validation-accuracy-estimation**: Use when comparing normalization methods on metabolomics abundance data by supervised classification performance — e.g. predicting hypertension status from BioHEART-CT plasma metabolite abundances — where a repeated, averaged CV accuracy estimate is needed per method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/repeated-cross-validation-accuracy-estimation))
- **repeated-cross-validation-accuracy-estimation**: Use when a binary phenotypic trait model (e.g. obligate intracellular vs free-living) trained on COG presence/absence vectors must be validated with a robust, replicate-averaged accuracy estimate rather than a single train/test split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/repeated-cross-validation-accuracy-estimation))
- **repeated-cross-validation-benchmarking**: Use when a labeled metabolomics or other small-n feature matrix must be benchmarked across several classifiers, and a stable estimate of held-out performance (accuracy, sensitivity, specificity, F1) under repeated k-fold cross-validation is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/repeated-cross-validation-benchmarking))
- **repeated-cross-validation-model-evaluation**: Use when a prediction task over high-dimensional feature tables (e.g., Deblur BIOM counts vs. EMPO labels) needs a rigorous out-of-fold accuracy estimate rather than a single train/test split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/repeated-cross-validation-model-evaluation))
- **repeated-cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantify downstream benefit of an adjusted expression matrix: sample-level labels exist (e.g., Healthy / Mild-Moderate / Severe-Critical), and you must compare predictive accuracy between adjusted and raw data per cell type. - **repeated-double-cross-validation-variable-selection**: Use when a preprocessed, imputed LC-MS metabolomics feature table (MetaboSet object) with a known class label (e.g. a 'group' column) is available and the goal is supervised ranking of molecular features into minimal-optimal vs. all-relevant subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/repeated-double-cross-validation-variable-selection))
- **repeated-explain-value-aggregation**: Use when you have a trained SIMS/TabNet classifier on single-cell RNA-seq data and need to quantify whether its sparse feature masks consistently select the same genes for a given cell type across independent explain runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/repeated-explain-value-aggregation))
- **repeated-failure-debug-escalation**: Stop a coding session that keeps retrying the same failing approach - log every attempt with its approach and error, count attempts per approach, stop at three, run a separate diagnostic pass that reproduces the failure and lists hypotheses before any new fix, and hand only verified findings back into the retry. Use when a fix has failed two or three times, the user says the agent is looping, or the same test or error returns after repeated changes. Not for planning remaining work (followup-wave-loop) or a first failed attempt. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/agent-session-craft/skills/repeated-failure-debug-escalation))
- **repeated-game-cooperation**: 长期重复关系（价格默契、供应商、平台商家、配额联盟）判断合作/背叛是否理性时使用：现值Σδ^(t-1)πt与平均收益概念+触发战略+门槛计算（囚徒4/(1−δ)≥5+δ/(1−δ)⇒δ≥1/4；古诺πm/2(1−δ)≥πd+δπc/(1−δ)⇒δ≥9/17；通用式δ≥max(di−xi)/(di−ei)）。Trigger：中"长期合作/价格战/报复"，英"is collusion sustainable/shadow of the future/grim trigger"。边界：证明合作可以是均衡而非必实现；非最严厉可信惩罚会高估门槛（阿布勒δ=1/2&lt;9/17）；帕累托劣惩罚可被重新谈判瓦解；偏离须可观察；δ外生难测。 ([raphael-y7/game-theory-primer-skill](https://github.com/raphael-y7/game-theory-primer-skill/tree/HEAD/pack/repeated-game-cooperation))

Use for within-subjects, longitudinal, or repeated-measurement designs with three or more time points. Evaluates subject-as-block variance partitioning, Greenhouse-Geisser sphericity epsilon ($\epsilon_{\text{GG}}$) corrections, F-statistics, and partial eta-squared effect sizes, persisting design matrices and Mauchly audit logs in SQLite. Use when analyzing repeated measures, longitudinal cohort trials, or within-subject condition effects. ([SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed](https://github.com/SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed/tree/HEAD/skills/STATISTICS/inferential/repeated-measures-anova))
